# Real anchored Gram construction using binary characters

Use gpt-6-astra/max, consult off. The fifth launch ended at max_rounds after
thirteen cumulative completed rounds. This SIXTH launch permits TWO further
rounds, cumulative maximum FIFTEEN. No optimization, numerical parameter
search, installation or routine fact_submit allowance. max3 alone owns its
separate direct-trajectory option; it is not transferable.

The zero-height and positive-height cancellation interval histories are
closed in0187e698076c31c3/e09fd8046ee1daf2. The forward-only residual repair
is refuted by an original earlier-pair inequality. Do not spend another wave
removing history or retuning a bounded-jump cancellation rule. The accepted
finite fact e9a55f57461f253c is outside even six-jump coordinate realizations,
though it does not exclude every useful low-jump witness at other horizons.

## New generating rule, not a change of basis in an old object

Explore an ACTUAL new gradient Gram rather than transform an existing one.
For n+1 chronological sample indices, assign distinct binary labels in
F_2^m with 2^m>=n+1. On dyadic horizons take the standard chronological
binary labels. The character chi_s(i)=(-1)^(s dot i mod2) is a REAL sign.
Choose explicit nonnegative weights w_s and positive amplitudes gamma_i,
and prescribe real gradients

    g_i=(gamma_i*sqrt(w_s)*chi_s(i))_s,
    K_ij=gamma_i*gamma_j*sum_s w_s*chi_s(i)*chi_s(j).

The weights/amplitudes must be a concrete scalable policy, not unconstrained
unknowns renamed as a solution. A level-structured nonuniform weight rule is
one starting point. Uniform weights merely recovering orthogonal gradients
are already too weak under accepted0e7e78056dc6258c. The constant character
can calibrate a Huber-like common-gradient boundary, but is not an improvement.
High-frequency characters can leave the old bounded-jump assumption; do not
claim that their REAL interpolation inequalities follow from finite-field
orthogonality or that chronological GD is invariant under label permutations.

For non-dyadic n, embedding labels does NOT add algorithm steps. Any eventual
all-h theorem must compare with the original B_n, not a padded horizon or
an unspecified constant-factor larger word. The adversarial construction may
depend on the whole predetermined h; it must define ONE function before its
trajectory, not adapt an oracle after seeing gradients.

## Anchored simplex interface and the real cost

Use C62 draft016467bc5c3eb666 as an explicit construction lead, not a verified
fact. It corrects the closest-distance normalization in the full Gram game.
For an independent gradient Gram K and common b_i=<g_i,X0>, with T_ii=1
and T_ij=h_j for j<i, the displayed normal conditions are

    b_j-b_i<=(T K)_ij-(T K)_ii,  b_i>=(T K)_ii,
    2F(X_n)=2b_n-2sum_(i<n)h_i K_in-K_nn,
    R^2=max_(u>=0)(2b^T u-u^T K u).

Re-derive the needed identities and all hypotheses in your own complete
object if used. Construct the common X0 and the ONE function whose gradient
is projection onto conv(0,g_0,...,g_n), or another equally explicit legal
realization. Retain every earlier, later and optimum normal/interpolation
comparison and the actual order of h. Do not assign independent function
values to characters. A PSD Gram alone is not a legal trajectory.

Compute or exactly bound the TRUE polar-cone distance, with a precise
complementarity certificate if using a proposed maximizer u. Do not replace
it by b^T K^(-1)b as an equality without justification. If a conservative
distance upper bound is used, label and pay its loss. Singular weights and
zero-step repetitions require an explicit treatment or an honest scope;
small perturbations do not preserve constants automatically.

## Target-specific deliverable

First establish a nontrivial actual legal family and an exact payoff law.
Test the excess-total-step cases where the universal Huber bound alone does
not reach1/B_n. Actual H_m is one diagnostic, not the whole affirmative goal.
A useful all-h construction must meet the exact benchmark uniformly in h
and sufficiently large n. A family only on a particular word does not do so.

Alternatively give a quantitative obstruction for the PRECISE new character-
kernel/amplitude policy that was tried and identify the constraint it fails.
A failed one-step point or an unmatched arbitrary full-Gram problem is not
a whole-family theorem. Keep any scope restriction explicit, especially a
fixed number of characters, conditioning, positive-only correlations or
source-order symmetry. Do not import the old normalized TENSOR DUAL-array
no-go as a theorem about this PRIMAL Gram; they are different objects.

The generic simplex universality reduction is itself equivalent to the full
problem at the supremum level and is not a profile proof. Progress here
requires explicit weights, amplitudes, common anchor and a quantitative law.
Publish complete shared evidence; an original-target solution still needs
independent attack and separately selected verification. No finalization.

not as an unrestricted W bound. Stop repeating their completed scope screens.

if it is written explicitly, uses m large enough for nonnegative steps and

also allowed; it cannot depend on the function or observed gradients.

Find explicit q_m and a full smooth-convex certificate for ALL functions and
dimensions, with limsup_m q_m B_(n_m)<1. This would resolve the original goal
negatively. The previously derived DRAFT comparison limsup B_n K_m<=2/e,
K_m=1/(2sum H_m+1), suggests one sufficient target q_m<=beta K_m with a proved
fixed beta<e/2; include/recheck that comparator proof rather than assuming it
as a fact. This sufficient bound is not necessary, so failing it does not
reject every possible asymptotic improvement.

Normalize L=1 and x_star=f_star=0 explicitly. The actual required identity is
  q_m||x0||^2-2f_n + sum_(i!=j) Lambda_ij Q_ij = M_m >=0,
with every Lambda_ij>=0, EVERY value coefficient canceled, and M_m PSD on the
FULL independent formal vectors x0,g0,...,g_n. Include the optimum index and

or an intrinsic-gradient-only V certificate, is insufficient for this task.
Accepted1252b8823bb5a290 permits exact unqueried-terminal re-selection but
retains all queried-prefix constraints. Do not delete earlier edges.

## Construction and calibration requirements

Seek an actual recursively specified multiplier/remainder family or another

terms. A horizon-dependent residual may be explored with its complete discrete
recurrence and uniform constants; a formal limiting differential equation is

to that ansatz or an actual legal lower witness, and distinguish those two

certificate. A generic PEP restatement is already known and is not a deliverable.

The only completion is the original uniform lower theorem or a rigorous fixed

consult OFF. ZERO mathematical program, numerical evaluation, arithmetic
replay, solver, executed recurrence, formal gate, installation or subagent.

three-checkpoint witness89e46fc6f580a48b/ed82149d6fbc7127. Do not extend
that same finite calibration by another checkpoint this round. Its full
history remains open, but this is a genuinely different combinatorial
hard-function search, as requested in the original Goal.

Investigate a projection-envelope hard function built from a SUBMODULAR
BASE POLYTOPE with rank/permutation structure, rather than a tree-leaf
simplex, fixed triangle or sum of squared directed-edge hinges. A concrete
starting family is the base polytope of a concave cardinality set function
(a permutahedral/orbit body); choose and freeze ONE exact dimension-indexed
rank profile after checking shared memory for any identical prior failure.

you use; audit all hypotheses. Main only retrieved this reference and

distance, and every actual original GD update. Smoothness normalization
must be paid. If the minimum set has lineality, use distance to that set,

must follow from the actual function, not just adjacent consistency.

for a diagnostic hierarchy H_(m,tau), with tau1/4 preferred because the

458: ## 5. 其他核心符号与对象
459: 
460: ### 5.1 质量、Huber 与 quadratic 通道
461: 
462: \[
463: S(H)=\sum_i h_i.
464: \]
465: 
466: Huber hard-instance 通道：
467: 
468: \[
469: \mathsf H(H)=\frac1{1+2S(H)}.
470: \]
471: 
472: 一维 full-curvature quadratic 通道：
473: 
474: \[
475: \mathsf Q(H)
476: =
477: \max_{0\le\lambda\le1}
478: \lambda\prod_{i=1}^n(1-\lambda h_i)^2.
479: \]
480: 
481: 二者都是 \(R_n(H)\) 的合法下界。
482: 
483: ### 5.2 JCY checkpoint 通道
484: 
485: 对 checkpoint path
486: 
487: \[
488: \mathcal T=\{t_1<\cdots<t_k\},
489: \qquad b_i=h_{t_i},
490: \]
491: 
492: 令
493: 
494: \[
495: z_0=2+\sum_{u<t_1}h_u,
496: \]
497: 
498: \[
499: z_i=2+\sum_{t_i<u<t_{i+1}}h_u,
500: \qquad 1\le i<k,
501: \]
502: 
503: \[
504: y=1+\sum_{u>t_k}h_u.
505: \]
506: 
507: 则 contract normalization 下
508: 
509: \[
510: J_{\mathcal T}
511: =
512: \frac1{2y}
513: \prod_{i=1}^k
514: \left(\frac{b_i}{2z_{i-1}}\right)^2.
515: \]
516: 
517: 空 path：
518: 
519: \[
520: J_\varnothing=\frac1{2(1+S)}.
521: \]
522: 
523: full checkpoint 通道：
524: 
525: \[
526: \mathsf J(H)=\max_{\mathcal T}J_{\mathcal T}.
527: \]
528: 
529: 三通道 hybrid：
530: 
531: \[
532: \mathfrak H(H)
533: =
534: \max\{\mathsf J(H),\mathsf Q(H),\mathsf H(H)\}.
535: \]
536: 
537: 注意：
538: 
539: \[
540: R_n(H)\ge\mathfrak H(H),
541: \]
542: 
543: 但 \(\mathfrak H(H)\) 很小不能推出 \(R_n(H)\) 很小。
544: 
545: ### 5.3 Fibonacci natural-cut counterfamily
546: 
547: \[
548: F_0=0,\quad F_1=1,\quad N=F_n,\quad n\ge5.
549: \]
550: 
551: rank-to-time permutation：
552: 
553: \[
554: t(r)
555: \equiv
556: (-1)^{n+1}F_{n-1}r
557: \pmod{F_n},
558: \]
559: 
560: residue \(0\) 代表真实 rank \(N\)，其逆排列记为 \(j_t\)。
561: 
562: 固定
563: 
564: \[
565: p=\frac{13}{10},\qquad a=\frac1{1000},
566: \qquad
567: h_t=a(N/j_t)^p.
568: \]
569: 
570: 定义
571: 
572: \[
573: P_t=2+\sum_{u<t}h_u,
574: \qquad
575: T_t=1+\sum_{u>t}h_u,
576: \]
577: 
578: \[
579: Z_{st}=2+\sum_{s<u<t}h_u.
580: \]
581: 
582: 为避免混淆，本报告用 \(\mathcal T\) 表示 checkpoint path，用 \(T_t\) 表示 terminal mass。

1587: 
1588: 暂将域扩到 \([0,\infty)^n\)。在盒 \([0,M]^n\) 内，统一于函数和维数，有
1589: \[

1591: \le2(1+M)^{2n-1}\|H-\widetilde H\|_1.

1593: 单位步长表给 \(V_n\le1\)；GS1 使低于 1 的子水平集满足
1594: \[

1596: \]
1597: 因此非负紧域取得最小值，连续性使其 infimum 与原正域一致。
1598: 
1599: 对任何取得最优的表，令
1600: \(C_H=\prod_i(1+h_i)\)。末尾追加单位步长，利用
1601: \(f(x_n)\le C_H\|g_n\|\) 得
1602: \[
1603: \boxed{V_{n+1}\le V_n-\frac{V_n^2}{4C_H^2}<V_n.}
1604: \]
1605: 最优表若含零步长，删除会违反严格递减。于是
1606: \[
1607: \boxed{\forall n\ge1\ \exists H_n^*\in(0,\infty)^n:
1608: R_n(H_n^*)=V_n.}
1609: \tag{GS2}
1610: \]
1611: 
1612: 甚至有显式内部盒：令 \(M_0=0\)、
1613: \[
1614: C_{n-1}=(1+M_{n-1})^{n-1},
1615: \quad\Delta_n=\frac1{4(2n-1)^4C_{n-1}^2},
1616: \quad L_n=2(1+M_n)^{2n-1},
1617: \quad\varepsilon_n=\Delta_n/L_n,
1618: \]
1619: 则每张真正全局最优表均满足
1620: \(\varepsilon_n\le h_i^*\le M_n\)。这些常数极差；它们排除边界逃逸，但不给可用的渐近增益。[S14，§3]
1621: 
1622: ## 13.3 GS3：任意前缀的最后一步 1 都可严格改善
1623: 
1624: 对正前缀 P，令 \(n=|P|+1\)、\(B=\prod_{h\in P}(1+h)\)、\(d=2n+1\)。取
1625: \[
1626: \delta=\frac1{16d^4B^4}.
1627: \]
1628: 源报告给出
1629: \[
1630: \boxed{
1631: R_n(P,1+\delta)
1632: \le R_n(P,1)-\frac1{256d^8B^6}
1633: <R_n(P,1).
1634: }
1635: \]
1636: 其证明以 \(x\) 为末步前点，\(g=\nabla f(x),z=x-g,a=\nabla f(z)\)，由余单调性
1637: \(\langle a,g\rangle\ge\|a\|^2\)，得到
1638: \[
1639: 2f(z-\delta g)\le2f(z)-2\delta\|a\|^2+\delta^2B^2.
1640: \]
1641: 再用统一距离界与 \(R_n(P,1)\ge d^{-2}\) 对接近最坏和远离最坏的函数分别控制。\(h\le1\) 的末步由单调性归入同一比较，因此
1642: \[
1643: \boxed{h_n^*>1\quad\text{对每张真正全局最优表成立}.}
1644: \tag{GS3}
1645: \]
1646: 
1647: W 表以 1 结尾，故不是固定 n 精确最优表；**改善量可能极小，不能据此排除其与最佳算法共享同一指数。**[S14，§4]
1648: 
1649: ## 13.4 联合优化和 KKT 的适用条件
1650: 
1651: 固定第 5 节 convention，原 minimax 等价于
1652: \[
1653: \min_{H,q,\lambda}q,
1654: \quad H>0,\quad\lambda\ge0,\quad\operatorname{div}\lambda=d_*,
1655: \quad Q_{dual}(H,q,\lambda)\succeq0.
1656: \]
1657: 它因 \(H\lambda\) 项而非凸。一般 PEP 算法参数优化已有 BnB-PEP 方法，不能把写出这个优化问题本身当作新理论突破。[L05]
1658: 
1659: 不能直接假定 \(R_n(H)\) 可微。S14 在这个光滑但非凸的锥约束问题上构造正常 KKT 乘子，其资格条件依赖以下严格 dual：
1660: 
1661: 给定 H，取 \(N=n+1\)、\(H_{max}=\max(1,h_i)\)、
1662: \(\theta=1/(4NH_{max})\)、\(w_i=\theta^{2i}\)。加入 star↔i 的等量循环流，得到纯梯度块；以 \(\operatorname{diag}(\theta^i)\) 合同归一化后，对角为 1、非对角行和 <1/8。先令全部边乘子严格正且满足 divergence，再放大这些循环流，最后增大 q，可使整个 \(Q_{dual}\succ0\)。
1663: 
1664: 在最优点保持 \(\Delta H=0\)，连向同 H 的严格 dual，就给锥约束的严格线性化方向。源报告据此使用 conic KKT；**独立审核必须核对完整等式约束的冗余处理、正常化乘子和此资格条件，不只看有限测试。**[S14，§6]
1665: 
1666: 令拉格朗日函数
1667: \[
1668: \mathcal L=q-\langle G,Q_{dual}\rangle
1669: +f^\mathsf T(\operatorname{div}\lambda-d_*)-\sum_{ij}\eta_{ij}\lambda_{ij}.
1670: \]
1671: 对 q、\(\lambda\) 的驻点和互补性给
1672: \[
1673: G\succeq0,\quad G_{00}=1,\quad I_{ij}=\eta_{ij}\ge0,
1674: \quad\lambda_{ij}I_{ij}=0,\quad Q_{dual}G=0,
1675: \quad q=2f_n.
1676: \]
1677: 因此得到的是合法 full-PEP 最优 primal，不是非 PSD 的 formal polar 数据。
1678: 
1679: ## 13.5 GS4：全时间切口正交和能量等式
1680: 
1681: 对 \(t=1,\ldots,n\)，定义
1682: \[
1683: \chi_t(i)=\mathbf1_{i\in\{t,t+1,\ldots,n\}},\qquad\chi_t(\star)=0,
1684: \]
1685: \[
1686: c_t=\sum_{i\ne j}\lambda_{ij}[\chi_t(i)-\chi_t(j)]g_j.
1687: \]
1688: 由
1689: \[
1690: \frac{\partial B_{ij}}{\partial h_t}
1691: =-[\chi_t(i)-\chi_t(j)]\operatorname{sym}(g_jg_{t-1}^\mathsf T)
1692: \]
1693: 和最优步长位于正开域，得到
1694: \[
1695: \boxed{\langle g_{t-1},c_t\rangle=0\quad(t=1,\ldots,n).}
1696: \tag{Cut}
1697: \]
1698: 
1699: 同一组相容数据还满足
1700: \[
1701: \boxed{2q x_0=\sum_j\lambda_{\star j}g_j,}
1702: \tag{Source}
1703: \]
1704: \[
1705: \boxed{q=\frac12\sum_{i\ne j}\lambda_{ij}\|g_i-g_j\|^2.}
1706: \tag{Energy}
1707: \]
1708: 
1709: 能量推导不丢历史：记 \(s=\sum_j\lambda_{\star j}g_j\)、
1710: \(\mathcal E_\lambda=\frac12\sum\lambda_{ij}\|g_i-g_j\|^2\)，精确展开
1711: \[
1712: \langle Q_{dual},G\rangle
1713: =q\|x_0\|^2-\langle x_0,s\rangle+\mathcal E_\lambda
1714: -\sum_t h_t\langle g_{t-1},c_t\rangle.
1715: \]
1716: 利用 complementarity、source、cut 得 \(q=\mathcal E_\lambda\)。这是**同一个全局 flow 和同一个合法 Gram**的能量，不是对不同局部证书作加法。[S14，§7]
1717: 
1718: ## 13.6 一步精确校准
1719: 
1720: \[
1721: h_1=3/2,\quad q=1/4,\quad f_0=1/4,f_1=1/8,
1722: \]
1723: \[
1724: G=\begin{pmatrix}1&1/3&1/6\\1/3&1/6&0\\1/6&0&1/12\end{pmatrix},
1725: \quad
1726: Q_{dual}=\begin{pmatrix}1/4&-1/2&-1/2\\-1/2&1&1\\-1/2&1&1\end{pmatrix}.
1727: \]
1728: 非零乘子 \(\lambda_{01}=\lambda_{\star0}=\lambda_{\star1}=1\)。有全部可行性、互补性，\(c_1=-2g_1\)、\(\langle g_0,g_1\rangle=0\)、\(\mathcal E_\lambda=q\)。正式程序从定义重构验证这些矩阵，而不只核对几个小数。[S14，§7.3]
1729: 
1730: ## 13.7 GS5：源、切口、能量三个简式不能代替 full PEP
1731: 
1732: 对任意 \(0<q<1/2\)，令
1733: \[
1734: x_0=\mathbf e_1,\quad g_0=q\mathbf e_1,
1735: \quad g_1=\sqrt{q-2q^2}\,\mathbf e_2,
1736: \quad h_1=1,
1737: \quad\lambda_{\star0}=\lambda_{01}=2.
1738: \]
1739: 它满足 source、cut 和 energy，却在 \(f_1=q/2\) 时有
1740: \[
1741: I_{\star1}=q^2-q<0.
1742: \]
1743: 所以任意小能量的这个“解”不是合法函数。**不能丢掉完整插值／PSD／互补关系，再只靠图能量不等式推最终指数。**[S14，§8]
1744: 
1745: ## 13.8 全域驻点系统与当前最具体的 open theorem
1746: 
1747: 定义 \(\mathfrak S_n\) 为同时满足以下条件的所有元组 \((H,q,G,f,\lambda)\)：
1748: 
1749: 1. \(H>0,\lambda\ge0,\operatorname{div}\lambda=d_*\)；
1750: 2. \(Q_{dual}(H,q,\lambda)\succeq0\)；
1751: 3. \(G\succeq0,G_{00}=1,f_*=0\)，全部 \(I_{ij}\ge0\)；
1752: 4. \(Q_{dual}G=0,\lambda_{ij}I_{ij}=0\)；
1753: 5. 全部 Cut 方程。
1754: 
1755: 源报告给出
1756: \[
1757: \boxed{V_n=\min\{q:(H,q,G,f,\lambda)\in\mathfrak S_n\}.}
1758: \tag{GS reduction}
1759: \]
1760: 每个元组都给同一 H 的 primal/dual matching；系统可包含非全局最优的 stationary 表，但全局最优至少在内，所以取最小仍是 \(V_n\)。
1761: 
1762: 当前缺失的 GSB 是
1763: \[
1764: \boxed{
1765: \exists c>0,\ \exists\beta<\alpha_*:\quad
1766: q\ge c(n+1)^{-\beta}\quad\forall n,\ \forall(H,q,G,f,\lambda)\in\mathfrak S_n.
1767: }
1768: \tag{GSB—OPEN}
1769: \]
1770: 证明它即可对**全部** H 推出相同下界；不必先证明任意 H 自己都满足 stationarity。不论原资料中曾如何强调“只差统一覆盖”，这里的 GSB 都仍是主要未解数学内容，不是最后一个机械步骤。

# 步长唯一加速梯度下降的 Silver 最优性猜想
## 系统研究报告与续研交接：严格模型、冻结结论、全局驻点、构造进展及失败路线

**整理日期：2026-09-05。**  
**版本：综合审计版 v4-GS（纳入 C1–C59、N60–N69、CG1–CG11、TC1–TC4、SH1–SH3、AR1–AR8、PR1–PR4、HB1–HB4、MP1–MP5、GS1–GS5）。**  
**主问题状态：`OPEN_GAP`。没有新的任意正步长表统一指数，没有证明 Silver，也没有证明 1.44 或 1.449984… 是真实最优指数。**  
**本文性质：对已提供研究材料的系统整理、状态协调和复现审计；不是新增全局定理，不提出未经检索的优先权主张。**

> **首先阅读这段。** 截至所提供材料的最新阶段，公开且本稿核实采用的范围仍是
> \[
> \boxed{\sigma:=\log_2(1+\sqrt2)\le p_{\rm NA}^\star\le
> \alpha_*:=\log_2(1+\sqrt3).}
> \]
> 已得到的 1.38848…、1.29278…、1.39970… 都带有不同的结构或函数类限制；不能替换上述右端。最新 GS 工作已经进入真正全局最优步长表的分析，但它只建立了必要条件系统，尚未从该系统推出新的幂次下界。
